\(\RollingShutterDistortion(\SensorRowIndex)\) Rolling Shutter Distortion \(\text{m}\) Kinematic skew displacement \(\RollingShutterDistortion = \Velocity \cdot \SensorRowIndex \cdot \RollingShutterRowReadout\) induced by high-velocity platform motion.
\(\Glyph{\text{tsdf}}(\VoxelCenterPosition)\) Truncated Signed Distance \([-1, +1]\) Normalized volumetric distance \(\Glyph{\text{tsdf}}(\VoxelCenterPosition) = \text{clamp}(\Glyph{d}(\VoxelCenterPosition)/\TsdfTruncationBand, -1, 1)\) of the voxel centered at \(\VoxelCenterPosition\) to the nearest surface.
\(\TsdfTruncationBand\) TSDF Truncation Band \(\text{m}\) Metric thickness of the spatial surface interface band stored in voxel memory.
\(\TsdfAccumulatedDistance_t(\VoxelCenterPosition), \TsdfAccumulatedWeight_t(\VoxelCenterPosition)\) TSDF Accumulated Volume Mixed Running weighted signed distance and accumulated visibility weight at the voxel centered at \(\VoxelCenterPosition\).
\(\InitialTrackingError, \PositionErrorCeiling\) Initial and Ceiling Error \(\text{m}\) Position error of an estimate when its evidence is fresh, and the largest error the task tolerates; \(\Glyph{E}_0\) and \(\Glyph{E_{\max}}\) stay energies.
\(\PositionalTaskTolerance\) Positional Task Tolerance \(\text{m}\) Radius within which a target estimate must lie for the task to succeed; paired with the orientation tolerance \(\OrientationTolerance\).
\(\GoalSet\) Geometric Goal Set Subset of \(\SpecialEuclideanGroup\) Admissible terminal poses within the positional and angular tolerances of the task; the spatial component of an intent lease \(\IntentLease\).
\(\TargetTravelDistance\) Distance to Target \(\text{m}\) Straight-line distance from the measured end-effector position to the admitted target, \(\lVert \TargetPosition - \CartesianPosition_0 \rVert\), in the intent-lease reachability test; distinct from the hidden width \(\HiddenDimension\).
\(\AttitudeError\) Attitude Error \(\text{rad}\) Angular error in an estimated attitude, which leaks gravity into the horizontal acceleration.
\(\AzimuthAngle\) Azimuth Angle \(\text{rad}\) Angle of a direction measured in the horizontal plane about the vertical axis; the book may state values in degrees.
\(\BaseCameraTransform\) Base-to-Camera Transform Matrix Rigid transform mapping camera coordinates into the robot base frame.
\(\BodyFramePoint\) Body-Frame Point \(\text{m}\) Position of a perceived point expressed in the robot body frame, the camera-frame point carried through the extrinsics.
\(\BodyFramePointCovariance\) Body-Frame Point Covariance \(\text{m}^2\) Covariance of a perceived point’s position error in the robot body frame, the spatial covariance an observation contract carries; rotated camera-frame covariance plus the extrinsic term, not the covariance of a state estimator.
\(\CameraExtrinsicRotation\) Camera Extrinsic Rotation Dimensionless Rotation part of a camera’s extrinsic calibration, taking camera-frame vectors into the body frame; the rotation block of \(\CameraExtrinsicTransform\).
\(\CameraExtrinsicTransform\) Camera Extrinsic Transform Matrix Rigid transform of a camera’s extrinsic calibration, mapping camera-frame coordinates into the body frame; mixed rotation and translation blocks.
\(\CameraFramePointCovariance\) Camera-Frame Point Covariance \(\text{m}^2\) Covariance of a point’s position error in the camera’s optical frame, after unprojection of its pixel and depth measurement; rotated into the body frame by \(\CameraExtrinsicRotation\).
\(\CartesianXCoordinate\) Cartesian X Coordinate \(\text{m}\) First Cartesian component of a three-dimensional position.
\(\CartesianYCoordinate\) Cartesian Y Coordinate \(\text{m}\) Second Cartesian component of a three-dimensional position.
\(\CartesianZCoordinate\) Cartesian Z Coordinate \(\text{m}\) Third Cartesian component of a three-dimensional position.
\(\ClearanceInset\) Clearance Inset \(\text{m}\) Separation a consumer holds between an estimated obstacle position and the machine’s envelope so the true obstacle stays the required clearance away, except with a stated tail probability; a field of the observation contract.
\(\DepthStandardDeviation\) Depth Standard Deviation \(\text{m}\) Standard deviation of the depth a stereo or range sensor reports for one point; for stereo it grows with the square of the range.
\(\DisparityStandardDeviation\) Disparity Standard Deviation pixels Standard deviation of the disparity a stereo matcher measures; it feeds the stereo depth term \(\DepthStandardDeviation\).
\(\ElevationAngle\) Elevation Angle \(\text{rad}\) Angle of a direction above the horizontal plane; not the encoder parameters of a variational autoencoder, which render the same glyph.
\(\ExtrinsicCalibrationCovariance\) Extrinsic Calibration Covariance \(\text{m}^2\) Covariance of a sensor’s calibrated mounting extrinsic.
\(\ExtrinsicMisalignmentAngle\) Extrinsic Misalignment Angle \(\text{rad}\) Angular error of a sensor’s extrinsic calibration relative to its true mounting.
\(\FocalLength\) Focal Length pixels Focal length of a pinhole camera in pixels, the scale from normalized image coordinates to pixels; a rectified pair shares one value.
\(\HorizontalFocalLength\) Horizontal Focal Length pixels Camera focal length in pixels along the horizontal image axis, one entry of the intrinsics.
\(\FrameExposureStartTime\) Frame Exposure Start Time \(\text{s}\) Instant at which the first row of a rolling-shutter frame begins its exposure.
\(\FrameReadoutTime\) Frame Readout Time \(\text{s}\) Time a camera takes to read its pixels out after exposure.
\(\EvidenceCaptureInstant\) Evidence Capture Instant \(\text{s}\) Instant, on a monotonic clock, at which the sensor evidence behind an observation or proposal was captured; observation age and proposal validity are measured from it.
\(\EvidenceEpoch\) Evidence Epoch \(\text{s}\) Capture instant of the most recent measurement that constrained a stored belief; republishing or predicting never advances it.
\(\FreshEvidenceTime\) Fresh Evidence Time \(\text{s}\) Capture time of the fresh physical evidence that renews a belief’s evidence epoch \(\EvidenceEpoch\).
\(\CurrentTime\) Current Time \(\text{s}\) Wall-clock time at which a consumer evaluates a stored record; evidence age is the current time minus \(\EvidenceEpoch\).
\(\DispatchObservationAge\) Dispatch Observation Age \(\text{s}\) Elapsed time from the sensor exposure midpoint to dispatch of the processed observation. Current consumer evidence age additionally includes elapsed time after dispatch.
\(\FreeConfigurationSpace\) Free Configuration Space Set Configurations at which the robot touches no obstacle, the configuration space minus the obstacle set.
\(\InverseDepth\) Inverse Depth \(\text{1/m}\) Reciprocal of a point’s depth along the optical axis, the parameterization that lets a landmark at infinity enter an estimator at zero; linear in disparity.
\(\ObstacleSeparationDistance\) Obstacle Separation Distance \(\text{m}\) Distance between the machine and the nearest obstacle or boundary at a given time, the signal a clearance predicate scores; written as a function of time.
\(\OccupancyLogOdds\) Occupancy Log-Odds Dimensionless Natural logarithm of the odds of a voxel’s occupancy probability, the quantity an occupancy map stores per cell; indexed by step, with the prior at step zero.
\(\MeasuredTrackedPosition\) Measured Tracked Position \(\text{m}\) Measured Cartesian position of the tracked point, which the tracking error compares against the reference.
\(\LastMeasurementTime\) Last Measurement Time \(\text{s}\) Capture time of the most recent measurement that updated a track or map cell.
\(\InitialVelocityStandardDeviation\) Initial Velocity Standard Deviation \(\text{m/s}\) Standard deviation of the velocity error of an estimate, here at the onset of occlusion.
\(\PixelStandardDeviation\) Pixel Standard Deviation Pixels Standard deviation of a measured image position, in pixels; the image-plane noise that propagates into depth and position error.
\(\PrincipalPointColumn\) Principal Point Column Pixels Horizontal pixel coordinate of the principal point, where the optical axis meets the image.
\(\PrincipalPointRow\) Principal Point Row Pixels Vertical pixel coordinate of the principal point, where the optical axis meets the image.
\(\StereoBaseline\) Stereo Baseline \(\text{m}\) Distance between the optical centers of a stereo camera pair; depth is focal length times baseline over disparity.
\(\StereoDisparity\) Stereo Disparity Pixels Horizontal pixel offset between the images of one scene point in the two views of a rectified stereo pair; not a travel distance or a sightline range, which share the glyph.
\(\TargetRange\) Target Range \(\text{m}\) Distance from a camera to the target it observes, measured along the viewing direction; stereo depth error grows with its square.
\(\TargetPosition\) Target Position \(\text{m}\) Position of the target an end effector is commanded toward, in the robot base frame; a decorated form of the Cartesian position.
\(\PositionErrorBound\) Position Error Bound \(\text{m}\) Worst-case position error of a target estimate as a function of the time elapsed since its evidence was captured; it grows while the target is occluded.
\(\PositionErrorEnvelope\) Position Error Envelope \(\text{m}\) Conservative bound on the position error of a target estimate along one coordinate axis of the base frame; written with the axis index.
\(\RangeObservation\) Range Observation Record One range observation a sensor returns at a time step; indexed by step, with the history as the sequence of them.
\(\SpatialMemoryMap\) Spatial Memory Map Set Occupancy map in spatial memory that stores the obstacle geometry around the machine; not a decision-process tuple.
\(\TransformRotationBlock\) Transform Rotation Block Matrix Rotation block of the homogeneous transform from frame B to frame A, its upper-left three-by-three part.
\(\TransformTranslationBlock\) Transform Translation Block \(\text{m}\) Translation block of the homogeneous transform from frame B to frame A, the position of the origin of frame B expressed in frame A.
\(\UnprojectionJacobian\) Unprojection Jacobian Mixed Jacobian of a camera-frame point with respect to its pixel coordinates and depth; it carries measurement covariance into Cartesian covariance. Not the manipulator Jacobian \(\ManipulatorJacobian\).
\(\VerticalFocalLength\) Vertical Focal Length Pixels Camera focal length expressed in pixels along the vertical image axis.
\(\WorldBodyTransform\) World-from-Body Transform Matrix Rigid transform giving the pose of the vehicle body frame in the world frame; it maps body coordinates into world coordinates.
\(\WorldFramePoint\) World-Frame Point \(\text{m}\) Position of an observed point expressed in the world frame; compare \(\CameraFramePoint\), the same point in the camera frame.
\(\PerceptionDepthCovariance\) Perception Depth Covariance \(\text{m}^2\) Covariance of the three-dimensional position error of a point lifted from stereo, lidar, or depth data; a matrix, not the scalar depth variance.
\(\VacuumLightSpeed\) Speed of Light in Vacuum \(\text{m/s}\) About \(3 \times 10^8\) m/s, the upper limit on how fast a signal crosses a chip or link; on-chip wires carry signals at a fraction of it.

Control, Observers, and Safety Filters

The control symbols follow state-space convention. A plant has a state, an input, and a measured output; an observer estimates the state from that output; and a safety filter, such as a control barrier function solved as a quadratic program, edits the input before it reaches the actuators.

Symbol Definition Unit Notes
\(\StateSpaceSystemMatrix, \StateSpaceInputMatrix\) Linear State-Space Matrices Matrix Continuous LTI plant: \(\PlantStateRate = \StateSpaceSystemMatrix\MechanicalPlantState + \StateSpaceInputMatrix\ControlInput + \ProcessNoiseVector\), observed output \(\MeasurementVector = \MeasurementMatrix\MechanicalPlantState + \MeasurementNoiseVector\) through the measurement map \(\MeasurementMatrix\) below.
\(\ProcessNoiseVector(\TimeInstant), \MeasurementNoiseVector(\TimeInstant)\) Process and Measurement Noise Vector Zero-mean Gaussian noise vectors with covariance matrices \(\ProcessNoiseCovariance \succeq 0\) and \(\MeasurementNoiseCovariance \succ 0\); bold \(\DirectionalCosineMatrix\) is reserved for rotation.
\(\StateTransitionMatrix, \MeasurementMatrix\) Transition and Measurement Maps Matrix Discrete state transition \(\PredictedStateEstimate = \StateTransitionMatrix\PreviousUpdatedStateEstimate\) and measurement map \(\MeasurementVector_k = \MeasurementMatrix\MechanicalPlantState_k + \MeasurementNoiseVector\) of a Kalman filter.
\(\KalmanGain_{k}\) Kalman Gain Matrix Weight on the innovation \(\MeasurementInnovation_k = \MeasurementVector_k - \MeasurementMatrix\PredictedStateEstimate\) in the update step; bold \(\CameraIntrinsicCalibrationMatrix\) is reserved for camera intrinsics.
\(\EstimationErrorCovariance \in \mathbb{R}^{\StateDimension \times \StateDimension}\) Estimation Error Covariance Matrix Symmetric positive-definite matrix \(\mathbb{E}[(\MechanicalPlantState - \StateEstimate)(\MechanicalPlantState - \StateEstimate)^\top]\) (\(\EstimationErrorCovariance(\TimeInstant)\) continuous, \(\UpdatedErrorCovariance\) discrete) evolving via matrix Riccati equation; \(\Glyph{\mathbf{P}_{\text{est}}}\) where an LQR cost matrix is also in scope.
\(\ObservabilityMatrix, \NonlinearObservationSpace\) Observability Matrices Matrix Kalman observability matrix \([\MeasurementMatrix^\top, (\MeasurementMatrix\StateSpaceSystemMatrix)^\top, \dots]^\top\) and nonlinear Lie matrix \([\DerivativeOperator\NonlinearOutputFunction, d\DriftLieDerivativeOperator\NonlinearOutputFunction, \dots]^\top\); calligraphic \(\mathcal{O}(\cdot)\) stays asymptotic cost. The dual controllability matrix is \(\ControllabilityMatrix\).
\(\ControllabilityMatrix\) Controllability Matrix Mixed Kalman block matrix formed from the input matrix and its products with powers of the system matrix; full row rank characterizes unconstrained LTI controllability.
\(\SafetyBoundaryManifold\) Safety Boundary Manifold Boundary Zero level set \(\{\MechanicalPlantState \mid \BarrierFunction(\MechanicalPlantState) = 0\}\), the boundary of the safe set \(\ForwardInvariantSafeSet\), separating nominal operation from irreversible hazard.
\(\BarrierFunction(\MechanicalPlantState), \BarrierFunction\) Control Barrier Function (CBF) Scalar Continuously differentiable function whose 0-superlevel set is the safe set \(\ForwardInvariantSafeSet\). Written \(\BarrierFunction(\MechanicalPlantState)\) on the plant state, and as the function head \(\BarrierFunction\) when it takes other arguments or is differentiated (\(\BarrierFunctionRate\), \(\DriftLieDerivativeOperator \BarrierFunction\), \(\BarrierFunction(\Glyph{\theta_{\text{clear}}}, \JointAngularSpeed)\)).
\(\ForwardInvariantSafeSet \subset \mathbb{R}^{2\GeneralizedCoordinateCount}\) Forward-Invariant Safe Set Set Superlevel set \(\ForwardInvariantSafeSet = \{\MechanicalPlantState \mid \BarrierFunction(\MechanicalPlantState) \ge 0\}\) of plant states where safety invariants are strictly maintained; a qualifier names a variant (\(\Glyph{\ForwardinvariantSafeSet}_{\text{contact}}\), \(\Glyph{\ForwardinvariantSafeSet}_{\text{discrete}}\)).
\(\ControlAffineDriftField(\MechanicalPlantState), \ControlAffineInputField(\MechanicalPlantState)\) Control-Affine Drift and Input Fields Vector, Matrix The control-affine model \(\PlantStateRate = \ControlAffineDriftField(\MechanicalPlantState) + \ControlAffineInputField(\MechanicalPlantState)\ControlInput\) of barrier analysis; these letters hold only inside that model, where the gravity torque \(\GravitationalTorqueVector(\GeneralizedCoordinates)\) and contact forces \(\ContactPointForce_{k}\) do not appear.
\(\LieDerivativeAlongDrift(\MechanicalPlantState)\) Lie Derivative along Drift Barrier units/s Directional gradient derivative \(\nabla \BarrierFunction(\MechanicalPlantState) \ControlAffineDriftField(\MechanicalPlantState)\) along unforced plant dynamics; its units are those of the barrier divided by time.
\(\LieDerivativeAlongControl(\MechanicalPlantState)\) Lie Derivative along Control Vector Directional control effectiveness gradient \(\nabla \BarrierFunction(\MechanicalPlantState) \ControlAffineInputField(\MechanicalPlantState)\).
\(\BarrierRelativeDegree\) Barrier Relative Degree Integer Number of differentiations required before control input \(\ControlInput\) appears (\(\InputLieDerivativeOperator \DriftLieDerivativeOperator^{\BarrierRelativeDegree-1} \BarrierFunction \neq 0\)).
\(\KineticInsetBarrierFunction(\MechanicalPlantState)\) Kinetic Inset Barrier Function Scalar Relative-degree-1 barrier: \(\KineticInsetBarrierFunction(\MechanicalPlantState) = \BarrierFunction(\MechanicalPlantState) - \BodySpeed^2 / (2 \CredibleBrakingDeceleration)\) incorporating stopping distance.
\(\ClassKFunction(\cdot), \ClassKFunction\) Class-\(\ClassKFunctionSet\) Rate Function Scalar function Extended class-\(\ClassKFunctionSet\) function in the barrier condition \(\BarrierFunctionRate \ge -\ClassKFunction(\BarrierFunction)\), written as the function head \(\ClassKFunction\) when applied; the book uses the linear choice \(\ClassKFunction(\BarrierFunction) = \LinearBarrierDecayRate \BarrierFunction\).
\(\LinearBarrierDecayRate\) Linear Barrier Decay Rate \(\text{s}^{-1}\) Coefficient of the linear class-\(\ClassKFunctionSet\) choice, \(\BarrierFunctionRate + \LinearBarrierDecayRate \BarrierFunction \ge 0\); bare \(\DiscountFactor\) is the reinforcement-learning discount factor.
\(\NominalControlCommand \in \AdmissibleControlSet\) Nominal Control Command \(\text{N}\cdot\text{m}\) / \(\text{rad}\) Unverified control candidate proposed across the proposal boundary by the learned policy.
\(\CertifiedControlAction \in \AdmissibleControlSet\) Certified Control Action \(\text{N}\cdot\text{m}\) / \(\text{rad}\) Privileged, safety-filtered control command resolved by the real-time active-set CBF-QP.
\(\ReferencePosition\) Reference Position \(\text{m}\) Commanded Cartesian position reference the plant tracks, \(\ReferencePosition(\TimeInstant)\); the feedback tracker bounds the tracked point’s position error by \(\lVert \CartesianPosition(\TimeInstant) - \ReferencePosition(\TimeInstant) \rVert \le \TrackingErrorBound\), with both positions expressed in the same frame. Whole-body clearance requires a declared output map or metric that bounds swept-volume displacement. Elsewhere bold \(\Glyph{\mathbf{r}}\) is local (a moment arm, ellipsoid semi-axes).
\(\BumplessTransferBlend(\BumplessTransferProgress)\) Bumpless Transfer Blend \([0, 1]\) \(\Glyph{C}^2\) quintic polynomial \(6\BumplessTransferProgress^5 - 15\BumplessTransferProgress^4 + 10\BumplessTransferProgress^3\) of normalized time \(\BumplessTransferProgress \in [0, 1]\), ramping between prior and candidate controllers.
\(\BumplessTransferBlendSlope\) Blend slope Dimensionless Derivative of the bumpless-transfer blend weight \(\BumplessTransferBlend\) with respect to normalized progress.
\(\BlendingTransitionWindow\) Blending Transition Window \(\text{s}\) Duration of bumpless transfer; for two fixed, latched acceleration commands, bounded by \(\Glyph{\tau_{\text{blend}, \min}} = 1.875 \lVert \FiniteChange \ControlInput \rVert / \JerkLimit\). Torque or position commands require their own compatible command-rate bounds.
\(\PidProportionalGain, \PidDerivativeGain, \PidIntegralGain\) PID / Feedback Gains Mixed Proportional, derivative, and integral gains for joint-level or operational-space error stabilization.
\(\LqrFeedbackGain, \LqrStateWeightMatrix, \LqrControlWeightMatrix\) LQR Gain and Weights Matrix State-feedback gain \(\CertifiedControlAction = -\LqrFeedbackGain\MechanicalPlantState\) and the state and control weights of the quadratic cost; the subscripts keep them distinct from intrinsics, process noise, and rotation.
\(\MpcPredictionHorizon\) MPC Prediction Horizon Control steps Number of predicted discrete control steps in the finite-horizon MPC formulation; distinct from physical-trial count and learned action-chunk horizon.
\(\VirtualDampingMatrix, \Glyph{\mathbf{K}_{\text{virt}}}\) Virtual Damping & Stiffness Matrices Matrix Target compliance matrices in impedance control: \(\Glyph{\boldsymbol{\tau}_{\text{safe}}} = -\VirtualDampingMatrix \GeneralizedVelocities - \Glyph{\mathbf{K}_{\text{virt}}} \FiniteChange\GeneralizedCoordinates + \GravitationalTorqueVector(\GeneralizedCoordinates)\).
\(\AccelerationNoiseSpectralDensity\) Acceleration Noise Spectral Density \(\text{m}^2/\text{s}^3\) Continuous power spectral density of the zero-mean white acceleration noise per axis in a constant-velocity motion model; sets how fast the predicted position uncertainty grows.
\(\AdmissibleDisturbanceSet\) Admissible Disturbance Set Set Set of disturbance values a falsification search may inject; the search is bounded to it.
\(\AdmittedControlCommand\) Admitted Control Command Per interface Control command the permission path applies to the actuators after admitting, clipping, or replacing a policy proposal; indexed by step. Not \(\NominalControlCommand\) (before the filter) or \(\CertifiedControlAction\) (the filter’s projection).
\(\AngularFrequency\) Angular Frequency \(\text{rad/s}\) Frequency of a sinusoidal signal, the free variable of a frequency response; evaluated at the crossover frequency when a delay’s phase lag is charged to the phase margin.
\(\BackwardEulerTransitionMatrix\) Backward Euler Transition Matrix Matrix Discrete-time transition matrix that backward (implicit) Euler integration produces, the inverse of the identity minus the system matrix times the step; unconditionally stable but numerically dissipative.
\(\BarrierConstraintBound\) Barrier Constraint Bound Varies Bound on the right side of one affine control-barrier constraint, one entry of \(\Glyph{\mathbf{b}_{\text{cbf}}}\); its unit follows the barrier function rate.
\(\BarrierConstraintNormal\) Barrier Constraint Normal Varies Normal vector of a single affine control-barrier constraint on the control input, equal to \(-\LieDerivativeAlongControl\) transposed; one row of \(\Glyph{\mathbf{A}_{\text{cbf}}}\).
\(\BarrierFunctionRate\) Barrier Function Rate \(\text{m/s}\) Time derivative of the control barrier function along the closed-loop dynamics, the rate in the barrier condition; a dot is not an allowed decoration on \(\BarrierFunction\), so it has its own entry.
\(\BarrierProjectionMultiplier\) Barrier Projection Multiplier Varies Lagrange multiplier of the barrier half-space constraint in the CBF-QP projection; starred at the optimum, where it is zero when the nominal command is already safe.
\(\BeliefErrorBound\) Belief Error Bound Varies Upper bound on the discrepancy between a stored belief and the true state after a given elapsed time without new evidence; the unit is that of the stored quantity (meters for a position, radians for an angle).
\(\BumplessTransferStartInstant\) Bumpless Transfer Start Instant \(\text{s}\) Instant at which a bumpless-transfer blend begins, the zero of its normalized progress; other chapters use the same letters for exposure or sweep starts, different quantities.
\(\ChannelCommand\) Channel Command Varies Scalar command of one actuator channel, such as the base acceleration or one joint torque; the unit is the channel’s command unit. Scalar per-channel form of \(\ControlCommand\).
\(\ClassKFunctionSet\) Class-K Function Set Set Class of continuous strictly increasing functions that are zero at zero; named in “extended class-K function.”
\(\ControlCommand\) Control Command Per interface Control command vector sent toward the actuators, whatever its source or stage; indices name instances. The stages are \(\NominalControlCommand\) and \(\CertifiedControlAction\).
\(\ControlInput\) Control Input Per interface Control input applied to the plant, the command a controller or safety filter sends to the actuators; units depend on the command interface (torques, setpoints).
\(\ControlInputDimension\) Control Input Dimension Count Number of components of the control input vector; not the mass \(\Mass\).
\(\ControlInputLowerLimit\) Control Input Lower Limit Per interface Per-component lower limit of the control input the actuators can deliver.
\(\ControlInputSet\) Control Input Set Set Set of control inputs the controller may choose from, a subset of the input space; the actuator box and the barrier-feasible subset are narrower sets inside it.
\(\ControlInputUpperLimit\) Control Input Upper Limit Vector Per-component upper limit of the control input the actuators can deliver.
\(\ControlStepIndex\) Control Step Index Dimensionless Position of one discrete control step, or setpoint, in a robot’s control timeline; a subscript on per-step quantities, bare in expressions such as a clipped next step.
\(\DerivativeGainMatrix\) Derivative Gain Matrix Matrix Derivative feedback gains acting on the tracking error rate in joint-space PD or PID control; its diagonal entries are the per-joint derivative gains.
\(\DifferentiabilityClass\) Differentiability Class Set Functions whose derivatives up to the order written as the superscript exist and are continuous; \(\DifferentiabilityClass^2\) means continuous position, velocity, and acceleration of a blended trajectory.
\(\DiscretePlantDynamics\) Discrete Plant Dynamics Function Discrete-time map from the current state and applied command to the next state; the deterministic counterpart of the plant’s transition kernel.
\(\DriftLieDerivativeOperator\) Drift Lie Derivative Operator Operator Rate of change of a scalar function of the state under the unforced dynamics; it is applied repeatedly for higher-order derivatives.
\(\DriftSpeedBound\) Drift Speed Bound \(\text{m/s}\) Bound on the unobserved speed at which a target drifts relative to its last measured position; not the bound on the state-estimate drift, \(\DriftVelocityBound\).
\(\DriftVelocityBound\) Drift Velocity Bound \(\text{m/s}\) Bound on the unmodeled velocity at which an unobserved estimate drifts from the true state.
\(\ConveyorSpeedBound\) Conveyor Speed Bound \(\text{m/s}\) Largest speed at which the takeaway conveyor carries a target; the bound used when sizing a catch window, not the belt speed \(\ConveyorSpeed\) of a single pick.
\(\EstimatedPlantState\) Estimated Plant State Mixed Estimate of the mechanical plant state reconstructed from sensor observations; its error covariance is a separate symbol.
\(\EstimatedPosition\) Estimated Position \(\text{m}\) Position of a point that a filter propagates under its nominal model.
\(\EstimatedVelocity\) Estimated Velocity \(\text{m/s}\) Velocity of a body that a filter propagates under its nominal model.
\(\FiniteTimeHorizon\) Finite Time Horizon \(\text{s}\) Length of the finite window from time zero to the horizon over which an optimization, rollout, test, or temporal-logic operator is evaluated; not the wall-clock time to complete a unit of work.
\(\ForwardEulerTransitionMatrix\) Forward Euler Transition Matrix Matrix Discrete-time transition matrix that forward Euler integration produces, identity plus the system matrix times the step; its spectral radius exceeds one for an undamped oscillator at any step size.
\(\HermiteBasisFunction\) Hermite Basis Function Function Cubic Hermite basis polynomial that weights an endpoint position or velocity in spline interpolation; indexed by two digits.
\(\ImaginaryUnit\) Imaginary Unit Dimensionless Square root of minus one, written \(\ImaginaryUnit\) by the engineering convention rather than \(\Glyph{i}\).
\(\InductiveVoltage\) Inductive Voltage \(\text{V}\) Voltage across a coil, the inductance times the rate of change of its current.
\(\InequalityConstraintFunction\) Inequality Constraint Function Varies Constraint function of an optimization problem, whose nonpositive values define the feasible set; it appears in the interior-point barrier.
\(\InputLieDerivativeOperator\) Input Lie Derivative Operator Operator Lie derivative along the input matrix field, mapping a scalar function of the state to its sensitivity to the control input.
\(\IntegrationTimeInstant\) Integration Time Instant \(\text{s}\) Time instant running over an integration interval, the dummy variable when the interval ends at the time \(\TimeInstant\); it keeps the integrand’s time distinct from the upper limit.
\(\IntegrationDummyTime\) Integration Dummy Time \(\text{s}\) Dummy time variable of integration inside a time integral, distinct from its limits; a primed time would be a decorated quantity, so this entry names the bound variable.
\(\LaplaceVariable\) Laplace Variable \(\text{1/s}\) Complex frequency variable of the Laplace transform, standing for differentiation in time; read as the imaginary unit times the angular frequency for sinusoidal motion.
\(\LargestEigenvalue\) Largest Eigenvalue Varies Largest eigenvalue of a symmetric matrix, applied to that matrix; its unit is that of the matrix entries.
\(\LqrCostToGoMatrix\) LQR Cost-to-Go Matrix Varies Symmetric solution of the regulator algebraic Riccati equation (the discrete one in discrete-time LQR), whose quadratic form is the optimal LQR cost-to-go; shares its glyph with the estimation error covariance.
\(\MpcControlSequence\) MPC Control Sequence Varies Sequence of control inputs over a model-predictive-control horizon, one per step, that the optimizer chooses; only the first is applied before the next re-solve.
\(\NonlinearOutputFunction\) Nonlinear Output Function Varies Output function of a nonlinear state-space model, mapping the state to the measurement vector \(\MeasurementVector\); not the barrier function nor the bias forces of the floating-base equations.
\(\OpenLoopTransferFunction\) Open-Loop Transfer Function Dimensionless Open-loop transfer function of a feedback loop, evaluated along the imaginary axis for frequency response; never the loss.
\(\PidProportionalGainMatrix\) PID Proportional Gain Matrix Varies Proportional gain matrix of multi-joint PD or PID feedback, the stiffness acting on the position error.
\(\MeasurementVector\) Measurement Vector Varies Measurement vector a sensor or detector reports at one step.
\(\MeasurementInnovation\) Measurement Innovation Varies Difference between a measurement and the measurement the predicted state implies; its normalized square is the NIS.
\(\MeasurementNoiseCovariance\) Measurement Noise Covariance Varies Covariance of the noise on a sensor measurement vector, such as a pixel position with depth or a Kalman filter measurement; units are those of the measurement squared.
\(\PathDelayAsymmetry\) Path Delay Asymmetry \(\text{s}\) Difference between the forward and reverse one-way delays of a clock-synchronization exchange, which biases the estimated clock offset by half its value; two-way time transfer assumes it is zero.
\(\PredictedStateEstimate\) Predicted State Estimate Vector State estimate at step \(\Glyph{k}\) predicted from measurements through the previous step, before the measurement update; the step index is part of the symbol.
\(\PredictedErrorCovariance\) Predicted Error Covariance Matrix Error covariance at step \(\Glyph{k}\) predicted from measurements through the previous step, before the update; the conditioned form of the estimation error covariance.
\(\PreviousUpdatedStateEstimate\) Previous Updated State Estimate Vector State estimate at the previous step after that step’s update, the input to the next prediction.
\(\PreviousUpdatedErrorCovariance\) Previous Updated Error Covariance Matrix Error covariance at the previous step after that step’s update, the input to the next prediction.
\(\ProcessNoiseCovariance\) Process Noise Covariance Matrix Covariance of the process noise vector; the predict step adds it to the error covariance.
\(\StateEstimate\) State Estimate Vector Estimate of the state vector that a recursive estimator maintains; conditioned forms with the step index baked in have their own symbols.
\(\ProportionalGainMatrix\) Proportional Gain Matrix Matrix Matrix of proportional feedback gains acting on the tracking error vector in joint-space PD or PID control; its diagonal entries are the per-joint proportional gains, and it is not the camera calibration matrix.
\(\SafeControlSet\) Safe Control Set Set Set of control inputs that satisfy every active barrier constraint and the actuator limits at the current state; it is empty when saturation leaves no command that keeps the barrier condition, which selects a fallback.
\(\SafeStateConstraintSet\) Safe State Constraint Set Set Set of plant states that satisfy the state constraints of a model predictive control problem, such as joint limits and obstacle margins; polyhedral, and not the barrier safe set.
\(\SafeSeparationDistance\) Safe Separation Distance \(\text{m}\) Smallest separation from an obstacle that a clearance requirement allows.
\(\SignalTrajectory\) Signal Trajectory Function Trajectory of a system’s real-valued signals over continuous time, the trace a temporal logic formula is evaluated on; two trajectories are told apart by subscripts.
\(\StlFormula\) Signal Temporal Logic Formula Record Signal temporal logic formula a signal must satisfy, such as a safety invariant or a reachability deadline; subscripts index subformulas.
\(\StlPredicateFunction\) Signal Temporal Logic Predicate Function Function Real-valued function of a signal whose sign decides an atomic temporal-logic predicate; its unit is the physical unit of that predicate’s margin, and it is indexed per predicate.
\(\StlRobustness\) Signal Temporal Logic Robustness Function Signed margin by which a signal satisfies (positive) or violates (negative) a temporal logic formula at a time; it carries the units of the atomic predicates when they agree and is dimensionless after a declared normalization. Not a correlation coefficient.
\(\SmallChange\) Small Change Operator Small change, increment or error in the quantity it prefixes; the companion of the finite change for first-order changes.
\(\SmallPerturbation\) Small Perturbation Operator Small change or error in the quantity it prefixes, treated to first order, as in a perturbation of a signal or a time; not a labeled localization bound that also renders \(\Glyph{\delta}\).
\(\PolynomialTrajectoryCoefficient\) Polynomial Trajectory Coefficient Varies Coefficient of one power of time in a polynomial joint trajectory segment, indexed by the power it multiplies; its unit is the joint unit divided by seconds to that power, and it is not an acceleration.
\(\SetpointSampleIndex\) Setpoint Sample Index Index Position of one discrete setpoint sample in a sampled trajectory.
\(\SetpointStepInterval\) Setpoint Step Interval \(\text{s}\) Time between consecutive setpoints of an action chunk, the spacing at which the controller consumes them; the reciprocal of the action execution rate.
\(\StateSpaceDimension\) State Space Dimension Dimensionless Number of continuous coordinates in the task-relevant state.
\(\StopCompletionInstant\) Stop Completion Instant \(\text{s}\) Instant, on the permission clock, at which the matched stop of an admitted trajectory record reaches rest; the commitment instant plus the stopping duration.
\(\StoppingDeadline\) Stopping Deadline \(\text{s}\) Latest instant at which authority can be revoked and a worst-case stop still ends within the verified clearance.
\(\PolicyLipschitzBound\) Policy Lipschitz Bound Varies Lipschitz bound of a learned policy over a stated operating domain, the largest ratio of action change to state change; it gives no guarantee unless established for that domain, and times the sensor error bound it bounds the action error.
\(\TaskToleranceSemiAxes\) Task Tolerance Semi-Axes \(\text{m}\) Principal semi-axes of the ellipsoid within which terminal position error must lie; the vector of \(\TaskToleranceSemiAxis\) values.
\(\TaskToleranceSemiAxis\) Task Tolerance Semi-Axis \(\text{m}\) Semi-axis of the task tolerance ellipsoid along one coordinate axis, written with the axis index.
\(\TerminalInvariantSet\) Terminal Invariant Set Set Plant states from which an invariant fallback control law keeps the system safe indefinitely, the required endpoint of a receding horizon. On an embodied system a validated stop that reaches rest within verified clearance realizes it.
\(\TrackingErrorVector\) Tracking Error Vector Vector Commanded reference state minus measured state of a feedback controller, acted on by \(\ProportionalGainMatrix\). Not the error feedback residual, which shares the glyph.
\(\TrackingErrorRate\) Tracking Error Rate Vector Time derivative of the tracking error vector, the velocity error of a feedback controller; multiplied by \(\DerivativeGainMatrix\) in PD control, so a step in it produces a torque step.
\(\UnitImpulseFunction\) Unit Impulse Function \(\text{s}^{-1}\) Dirac delta, the idealized unit impulse of zero width and unit area; with time as its argument it carries inverse seconds.
\(\UpdatedErrorCovariance\) Updated Error Covariance Matrix Error covariance at a step after incorporating that step’s measurement, the posterior counterpart of \(\EstimationErrorCovariance\).
\(\UpdatedStateEstimate\) Updated State Estimate Vector State estimate at a step after incorporating that step’s measurement, the posterior estimate of a Kalman filter.
\(\DerivativeOperator\) Derivative Operator Operator Differential operator of an ordinary derivative, written over the differential in a Leibniz quotient. Prefer writing the derivative so the \(\Glyph{d}\) sits before a variable.
\(\FiniteChange\) Finite Change Operator Finite change in the quantity it prefixes, between two states, configurations, or points in time. Differs from the increment fused into named entries such as \(\JouleanTemperatureRise\).
\(\PathCoordinate\) Path Coordinate Dimensionless Scalar coordinate along a geometric path, from zero at its start to one at its end, that a timing law maps to time; not a Laplace variable.

Learned Policies for Robotics

Learned policies propose actions rather than issue them. The symbols below cover how those policies are trained and sampled, including diffusion denoising, action chunking, and vision-language-action models, and how their imitation and reinforcement-learning objectives are written.

Symbol Definition Unit Notes
\(\Policy\) Policy Distribution Deep policy network parameterized by weights \(\ModelParameters\) (italic), mapping observation tensors to action distributions.
\(\MultimodalObservation_{t}\) Multimodal Observation Mixed Synchronized tokenized sensor packet (RGB images, depth latents, joint encoders, tactile arrays).
\(\CognitivePolicyAction_{t} \in \ActionSpace\) Cognitive Policy Action Normalized Unprivileged action emitted by neural policy \(\Policy\) (normalized joint delta, target pose, or gripper command).
\(\EnsemblingWindowLength\) Ensembling Window Length Time steps Number of overlapping past chunks blended in receding horizon execution (\(\EnsemblingWindowLength \le \ActionChunkHorizon\)).
\(\DecoderEvaluationCount\) Decoder Evaluation Count Count Dependent forward passes of a generative action head per chunk (denoising or ODE-solver steps); distinct from the ensembling window \(\EnsemblingWindowLength\) and the step index \(\DiffusionStepIndex\).
\(\LatentStyleVariable \in \mathbb{R}^{\LatentDimension}\) Latent Style / State Embedding Dimensionless Compressed semantic representation or CVAE latent style variable in ACT policies.
\(\DiffusionCumulativeSchedule_{k}\) Diffusion Schedule Parameter \([0, 1]\) Cumulative noise variance schedule parameter \(\DiffusionCumulativeSchedule_{k} = \prod_{\Glyph{s}=1}^{\DiffusionStepIndex} (1 - \Glyph{\beta}_{\Glyph{s}})\) at diffusion step \(\DiffusionStepIndex\).
\(\DiffusionGaussianNoise \sim \GaussianDistribution(\ZeroVector, \IdentityMatrix)\) Diffusion Gaussian Noise Tensor Standard normal Gaussian noise corrupted onto clean action trajectories.
\(\NoisePredictionNetwork(\MultimodalObservation_{t}, \NoisedActionChunk_t, \DiffusionStepIndex)\) Noise Prediction Network Tensor Neural network predicting added noise in Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Models (DDPM).
\(\NoisedActionChunk\) Noised Action Chunk Tensor Action chunk after \(\DiffusionStepIndex\) forward-diffusion steps, \(\sqrt{\DiffusionCumulativeSchedule_{k}}\,\ActionChunk^0 + \sqrt{1 - \DiffusionCumulativeSchedule_{k}}\,\DiffusionGaussianNoise\); the denoising iterate the reverse process refines.
\(\DiffusionMatchingLoss(\ModelParameters)\) Diffusion Matching Loss Scalar Mean-squared error objective: \(\mathbb{E}[\lVert \DiffusionGaussianNoise - \NoisePredictionNetwork(\MultimodalObservation_{t}, \NoisedActionChunk_t, \DiffusionStepIndex) \rVert^2]\) optimized across denoising steps.
\(\ActVariationalObjective(\ModelParameters, \CvaeEncoderParameters)\) ACT Variational Objective Scalar CVAE objective combining trajectory reconstruction \(\Glyph{\mathcal{L}_{\text{recon}}}\) with latent KL-regularization \(\LatentKlWeight \KlDivergence\).
\(\CvaeRecognitionEncoder(\LatentStyleVariable \mid \MultimodalObservation, \ActionChunk)\) CVAE Recognition Encoder Distribution Variational encoder in Action Chunking with Transformers parameterizing latent distribution \(\GaussianDistribution(\GaussianMeanVector, \GaussianCovarianceMatrix)\); \(\CvaeEncoderParameters\) here is the encoder’s parameter set.
\(\DivergenceInstant\) Divergence Instant \(\text{s}\) Time at which the autonomous policy’s rollout begins to leave its supported states before a takeover; the start of the interval excluded from imitation targets.
\(\TrajectoryDivergenceResidual\) Trajectory Divergence Residual \(\text{m}\) Signed lateral gripper-position error between measured and planned kinematics in the declared fixture frame for the latch example.
\(\DivergenceTolerance\) Divergence Tolerance \(\text{m}\) Positive threshold for the declared trajectory-divergence residual; expressed in that residual’s physical unit.
\(\Reward\) Reward Scalar Per-step reward \(\Reward(\PhysicalWorldState, \CognitivePolicyAction)\) that policy search maximizes; the stage cost of control is its negative. The barrier relative degree \(\BarrierRelativeDegree\) also renders \(\BarrierRelativeDegree\) but appears only in barrier analysis.
\(\ResidualGate\) Residual Gate \(\{0, 1\}\) Deterministic, independently evaluated gate \(\ResidualGate(\PhysicalWorldState)\) that admits a learned residual correction only inside the certified contact region and is zero elsewhere.
\(\TrialCount\) Trial Count Trials Number of physical trials (rollouts) in a learning or evaluation campaign, which sets its wall-clock cost; distinct from the zero-failure trial count \(\Glyph{n}\).
\(\SingleStepImitationError\) Single-Step Imitation Error \([0, 1]\) Bounded probability of an unforced policy error during an individual execution step; bold \(\DiffusionGaussianNoise\) is diffusion noise, the conformal miscoverage rate is \(\ConformalMiscoverageRate\), and other error bounds carry a subscript (\(\TrackingErrorBound\)).
\(\BehaviorCloningHorizon\) Behavioral-Cloning Horizon Control steps Episode length in control steps over which imitation error compounds; not the action-chunk horizon \(\ActionChunkHorizon\), not a duration \(\Glyph{T}\), and not the token sequence length \(\SequenceLength\).
\(\CovariateDriftBound\) Compounding Covariate-Drift Bound Task cost Worst-case excess cost of fixed-archive behavioral cloning, which grows quadratically over a rollout of \(\BehaviorCloningHorizon\) control steps with per-step error \(\SingleStepImitationError\).
\(\VitPatchProjectionWeights, \Glyph{\mathbf{E}_{\text{pos}}}\) ViT Projection & Positional Bias Matrix Convolutional/linear patch projection matrix and spatial coordinate position embedding tensors.
\(\MultimodalFusedTokenStream\) Multimodal Fused Token Stream Tensor Concatenated token sequence \([\VisionTokenEmbeddings, \TextTokenEmbeddings, \ProprioceptiveTokenEmbeddings]\) ingested by embodied transformer backbones.
\(\KvCacheMemory\) KV-Cache Memory bytes Autoregressive key-value cache memory footprint at the embodied batch size \(\BatchSize = 1\) (\(2 \times \LayerCount \times \HiddenDimension \times \SequenceLength \times \ElementStorageSize\) with full multi-head attention); the core’s quantity.
\(\IntentLease\) Intent Lease Tuple \((\GoalSet, [\LeaseAdmissionInstant, \IntentLeaseExpiry], \ContactWrenchCeiling)\): the admitted goal set, its validity interval, and its effort ceilings; it names a target, never a path. Calligraphic \(\LossFunction\) stays the loss.
\(\IntentLeaseExpiry\) Intent-Lease Expiry \(\text{s}\) \(\IntentLeaseExpiry = \min(\TimeInstant_0 + \EvidenceHorizon,\, \LeaseAdmissionInstant + \IntentAdministrativeLease,\, \Glyph{t_{\text{deadline,stop}}})\), where \(\Glyph{t_{\text{deadline,stop}}}\) is the instant by which a stop must begin; see the lease table above.
\(\EvidenceHorizon\) Evidence Horizon \(\text{ms}\) Time for which a target estimate stays within its task tolerance without fresh evidence.
\(\ConformalPredictionSet\) Conformal Prediction Set Set Calibrated set of referent candidates that cannot be excluded at miscoverage rate \(\ConformalMiscoverageRate\); a singleton admits the referent, and a larger set refuses. It shares the glyph of the safe set \(\ForwardInvariantSafeSet\), but the two never appear together.
\(\ConformalMiscoverageRate\) Conformal Miscoverage Rate Probability Allowed marginal probability that the conformal prediction set misses the true referent under the calibration procedure’s exchangeability assumptions; coverage is at least \(1 - \ConformalMiscoverageRate\).
\(\ExternalWait\) External Wait Seconds Time the machine spends waiting for a remote operator’s response during a handover or intervention.
\(\AggregatedSurrogateLoss\) Aggregated Surrogate Loss Dimensionless Best-in-class surrogate imitation loss on the states the learner visited, aggregated over the rounds so far; differs from the error measured on expert-visited states.
\(\AggregationLearnedPolicy\) Aggregation Learned Policy Function Policy trained in one round of corrective dataset aggregation, one of the sequence the bound selects from; carries a round index in the appendix.
\(\ChunkExecutionHorizon\) Chunk Execution Horizon Count Number of actions of each predicted chunk the controller executes before the policy is queried again; at most the chunk horizon.
\(\ChunkStepIndex\) Chunk Step Index Index Position of one step within an action chunk, counted from zero.
\(\ConservativePenaltyWeight\) Conservative Penalty Weight Dimensionless Weight of the conservative penalty that pushes down values of unsupported actions in conservative Q-learning.
\(\ContinuousActionSpace\) Continuous Action Space Set Set of continuous actions a robot policy may emit, a subset of real space of the action dimension.
\(\ContinuousActionVector\) Continuous Action Vector Varies Continuous action vector of the robot’s decision process, written without a step index: the argument of a policy, value function, reward, or action distribution, or an action recorded from a demonstrator. A star marks the demonstrated action; it lives in \(\ContinuousActionSpace\).
\(\CriticActionReturnEstimate\) Critic Action-Value Estimate Function Return a learned critic predicts for a state and an action; the action counterpart of the critic’s state-value estimate.
\(\DecoderPassTime\) Decoder Pass Time \(\text{s}\) Time of one dependent forward pass of a generative action head, one denoising or ODE-solver step; summed over the decoder evaluations in the proposer inference time.
\(\DemonstrationActionDistribution\) Demonstration Action Distribution Distribution Conditional distribution of the actions demonstrators took given an observation, the target a behavioral cloning decoder models; its mean minimizes squared error and can fall between modes (mode averaging).
\(\DemonstrationDataset\) Demonstration Dataset Set Recorded observation-action pairs that an imitation loss averages over.
\(\EmpiricalTimestepAverage\) Empirical Timestep Average Operator Sample average over the timesteps of a batch of rollouts, the estimate of an expectation.
\(\EnsemblingDecayRate\) Ensembling Decay Rate Dimensionless Decay factor per chunk index of exponential temporal-ensembling weights; its size sets how fast newer chunks enter the blend.
\(\ImitationLearnedPolicy\) Imitation-Learned Policy Function Policy learned from expert demonstrations or expert-labeled states, compared against the expert policy; indexed by aggregation round in DAgger.
\(\LatentKlWeight\) Latent KL Weight Dimensionless Weight on the KL divergence between the latent posterior and prior in a conditional variational autoencoder objective; not the RLHF reference penalty coefficient.
\(\LatentPosteriorDistribution\) Latent Posterior Distribution Distribution Distribution over the latent style variable that a conditional VAE encoder infers from a demonstration chunk and its observation; compared with \(\Glyph{p}\) in the KL term.
\(\MixtureComponentCount\) Mixture Component Count Dimensionless Number of components of a mixture distribution.
\(\MixtureComponentIndex\) Mixture Component Index Index Position of one component of a mixture distribution, from one to the component count \(\MixtureComponentCount\); it indexes the weight, mean, and covariance of a mixture density network.
\(\MixtureComponentMean\) Mixture Component Mean Varies Mean vector of one component of a Gaussian mixture, output by a mixture density network for the given input.
\(\MixtureComponentCovariance\) Mixture Component Covariance Varies Covariance matrix of one component of a Gaussian mixture, output by a mixture density network for the given input.
\(\ObservationEncodingTime\) Observation Encoding Time \(\text{s}\) Time to encode one observation, visual backbone and proprioception, once per policy proposal; the first term of the proposal latency.
\(\ObservationHistoryWindow\) Observation History Window Varies Sliding window of the robot’s most recent onboard observations, from the history length before the current step up to the current step; one row per observation step.
\(\ObservationHistoryLength\) Observation history length Steps Number of past steps the observation history window holds before the current one.
\(\RobotAction\) Robot Action Vector Action vector a learned robot policy emits, written without a step index as the argument of a policy, value function, or action distribution; the same quantity as the cognitive policy action, which carries the step index.
\(\RobotObservation\) Robot Observation Vector Observation vector a robot policy receives from its sensors, written without a step index.
\(\RequestedActionTap\) Requested Action Tap Record First stream of the four-stream action tap, the raw policy or operator request before kinematic mapping.
\(\SuccessorActionVector\) Successor Action Vector Vector Continuous action vector taken in the successor state of a transition, the next action a Bellman backup evaluates.
\(\ReturnStepOffset\) Return Step Offset Index Number of control steps after the current step at which a reward arrives, the summation index of a discounted return.
\(\RolloutHorizon\) Rollout Horizon Dimensionless Number of control steps in one executed rollout, the index of its final state; a step count, not a duration.
\(\RolloutPolicyParameters\) Rollout Policy Parameters Vector Parameters of the policy that collected the current batch of rollouts, frozen while the updated parameters are optimized; the denominator policy of the probability ratio.
\(\PpoClippedSurrogateObjective\) PPO Clipped Surrogate Objective Dimensionless Clipped surrogate objective that proximal policy optimization maximizes, the empirical mean of the smaller of the ratio-weighted advantage and its clipped counterpart; a whole-batch objective, not the per-token term of the language model variant.
\(\PrivilegedSimulatorState\) Privileged Simulator State Record Ground-truth simulator quantities such as friction and contact wrenches that a deployed policy cannot measure; a subset of the physical world state exposed by the simulator.
\(\SimulationParameterVector\) Simulation Parameter Vector Varies Physical parameters that define one simulated environment: link masses, centers of mass, friction, joint damping, transport delays, and sensor noise; sampled in domain randomization, with the nominal point marked by a zero subscript.
\(\ProprioceptiveTokenEmbeddings\) Proprioceptive Token Embeddings Vector Token embeddings of the robot’s joint and force state fed to an embodied transformer; one block of the fused multimodal token stream.
\(\StaticDataDistribution\) Static Data Distribution Distribution Fixed data distribution from which a static benchmark draws its held-out examples.
\(\TextTokenEmbeddings\) Text Token Embeddings Vector Token embeddings of the language instruction fed to an embodied transformer, one block of the fused token stream.
\(\VisionTokenEmbeddings\) Vision Token Embeddings Vector Token embeddings an embodied transformer computes from camera images, one block of the fused token stream.
\(\VitPatchSize\) Patch Size Pixels Side length of one square image patch a vision transformer tokenizes, typically 14 or 16 pixels.
\(\VitPatchTokenEmbedding\) Patch Token Embedding Vector Latent token embedding a vision transformer forms from one image patch.
\(\CriticReturnEstimate\) Critic Return Estimate Dimensionless Remaining-return estimate a learned critic network predicts for an interaction history; the baseline of PPO, which GRPO replaces with the group mean reward.
\(\DecisionProcessState\) Decision Process State Record Complete environment state of a decision process, including interaction history and variables the agent cannot observe. A record, not a number.
\(\DeviationDisadvantage\) Deviation Disadvantage Dimensionless Largest extra expected cost of one deviation from the reference action followed by reference recovery, at any state the learner can reach; an assumption about the task, in normalized cost units.
\(\DiscountFactor\) Discount Factor Dimensionless Factor by which a reward arriving one step later is weighted in the return, between zero and one; one means undiscounted.
\(\EnvironmentTransitionDistribution\) Environment Transition Distribution Distribution Distribution the environment induces over the next state given the current state and the action taken.
\(\EstimatedHistoryAdvantage\) Estimated History Advantage Dimensionless Estimate of how much an action taken after a given interaction history beats the policy value at that history.
\(\ExpectedHistoryActionReturn\) Expected History-Action Return Function Expected remaining return under a policy after taking a given action at a given interaction history and then following the policy.
\(\ExpectedRemainingReturn\) Expected Remaining Return Function Expected remaining return under a policy from a given interaction history at a decision step; written with the policy as a superscript.
\(\ExpectedReturnObjective\) Expected Return Objective Function Expected return of a policy as a function of its parameters, the objective reinforcement learning maximizes. Not the imitation cost sum \(\ExpectedTaskCostSum\).
\(\ExpectedTaskCostSum\) Expected Task Cost Sum Function Expected sum over the horizon of the normalized immediate task costs a policy incurs; the imitation bounds compare it for a learner and the reference. Not monetary.

Embedded Real-Time Systems, Power, and Hardware Safety

Response-time analysis keeps the Liu–Layland letters of its literature, but only inside that model and only with a task index \(\Glyph{i}\), \(\Glyph{j}\), or \(\Glyph{k}\). The letters are \(\WorstCaseExecutionTime_i\) (worst-case execution time), \(\TaskBlockingTime_i\) (blocking), \(\TaskInterferenceTime_i\) (interference), \(\Glyph{J}_i\) (release jitter), \(\TaskPeriod_i\) (period), \(\WorstCaseResponseTime_i\) (worst-case response time), and \(\TaskRelativeDeadline_i\) (relative deadline, which is \(\CompletionDeadline\) of task \(\Glyph{i}\) in the series’ terms). They combine as in \(\WorstCaseResponseTime_i = \WorstCaseExecutionTime_i + \TaskBlockingTime_i + \TaskInterferenceTime_i \le \TaskRelativeDeadline_i\). Outside that model these letters keep their table meanings.

Symbol Definition Unit Notes
\(\PowerRailInductance, \PowerRailResistance\) Power Rail Inductance / ESR \(\MicroUnitPrefix\text{H}, \text{m}\OhmUnit\) Parasitic inductance and equivalent series resistance of the power harness feeding the inverter; with \(\DcBusBulkCapacitance\) they set the droop \(\Glyph{\Delta V_{\text{droop}}}\) under a current step.
\(\DcBusBulkCapacitance\) DC Bus Bulk Capacitance \(\MicroUnitPrefix\text{F}\) Inverter decoupling capacitor bank buffering transient inductive voltage collapse.
\(\MemoryBusQueueDelay\) Memory Bus Queue Delay \(\text{ms}\) Contention latency \(\sum_{\Glyph{k}} {\DataVolume}_{\Glyph{k}} / \Glyph{\text{BW}_{\text{peak}}} + \Glyph{N_{\text{conflict}}} \BankConflictPenalty\) across shared SoC crossbars.
\(\TelemetryFreshnessBound\) Telemetry Freshness Bound \(\text{ms}\) Maximum allowable age of sensor telemetry before an interlock trips: \(\TelemetryFreshnessBound \le \PositionErrorCeiling / \SpeedBound\).
\(\ClockOffset\) Clock Offset \(\MicroUnitPrefix\text{s}\) Offset of one clock from another, estimated from a timestamp exchange as \(\ClockOffset = [(\TimeInstant_2 - \TimeInstant_1) - (\TimeInstant_4 - \TimeInstant_3)]/2\); its one-way path delay is \(\PtpPropagationDelay\).
\(\TimingJitter\) Timing Jitter \(\MicroUnitPrefix\text{s}\) Standard deviation of a nominally periodic event’s timing about its schedule.
\(\SynchronizationBlackout\) Synchronization Blackout \(\text{s}\) Interval during which a node receives no synchronization message and free-runs on its own clock.
\(\AcquisitionEpoch\) Acquisition Epoch \(\text{s}\) Instant a sensor transduced one observation, such as a camera frame's exposure midpoint or an encoder latch; the evidence epoch of a belief is the latest acquisition epoch among the measurements that constrained it.
\(\ActuatorOffTime\) Actuator Recovery Time \(\text{s}\) Recovery duration between actuator bursts in one duty cycle; the burst itself is \(\ActuatorOnTime\).
\(\ActuatorOnTime\) Actuator Burst Time \(\text{s}\) Duration an actuator runs at burst power in one duty cycle; pairs with the recovery time \(\ActuatorOffTime\).
\(\AisleTravelSpeed\) Aisle Travel Speed \(\text{m/s}\) Speed the mobile base is set to travel in a warehouse aisle, chosen below the derived dry-floor speed ceiling; it is the speed at detection in the aisle stopping budget.
\(\ArrivalTimestamp\) Arrival Timestamp \(\text{s}\) Instant a sensor sample arrives in host memory, when its direct memory access transfer completes; later than the capture timestamp by exposure, readout, transport, and transfer.
\(\AvailableBrakingDistance\) Available Braking Distance \(\text{m}\) Clear distance minus the localization bound and the protective clearance margin; the distance braking and blind travel may use.
\(\AvailableDeceleration\) Available Deceleration \(\text{m/s}^2\) Deceleration a machine actually has, the smaller of its credible braking deceleration and the traction limit, friction coefficient times \(\Gravity\).
\(\BankConflictPenalty\) Bank Conflict Penalty \(\text{s}\) Time one memory bank or row conflict adds to a queued transfer, about a precharge plus an activation.
\(\BlindTravelDistance\) Blind Travel Distance \(\text{m}\) Distance a machine travels during the pre-brake delay, before retarding force begins: speed at detection times that delay.
\(\BrakeChopperResistance\) Brake Chopper Resistance \(\OhmUnit\) Resistance of the absorption resistor a dynamic brake chopper switches across the bus; must dissipate the peak regenerated power at the clamp voltage.
\(\BrakingDistance\) Braking Distance \(\text{m}\) Distance a machine travels from brake onset to rest, speed squared over twice the credible deceleration; excludes travel during the pre-brake delay.
\(\CaptureTimestamp\) Capture Timestamp \(\text{s}\) Midpoint of the sensor’s exposure or firing window, the instant the observation describes; the letter t carrying index zero.
\(\CaseTemperature\) Case Temperature \(^\circ\text{C}\) Temperature of a motor or package case.
\(\CaseThermalTimeConstant\) Case Thermal Time Constant \(\text{s}\) Thermal time constant of the motor case stage of a multi-node motor thermal model.
\(\ChopperClampVoltage\) Chopper Clamp Voltage \(\text{V}\) Bus voltage at which a dynamic brake chopper switches its absorption resistor across the bus; above the full-charge voltage and below the converter and switch ratings.
\(\ClockConversionBound\) Clock Conversion Bound \(\text{s}\) Bound on the error of converting a sensor’s capture timestamp to the clock of the safety microcontroller that hosts the permission path; measured from offset tails, path asymmetry, and drift, and added to the observation age at dispatch.
\(\ClockConversionIntercept\) Clock Conversion Intercept \(\text{s}\) Intercept of the affine mapping that converts a capture-clock time to the safety microcontroller’s clock.
\(\ClockConversionSlope\) Clock Conversion Slope Dimensionless Slope of the affine mapping that converts a capture-clock time to the safety microcontroller’s clock; near one, the drift between the clocks.
\(\ConsoleCommunicationTime\) Console Communication Time \(\text{s}\) Time the operator console and link add to a takeover: video transport to the operator plus the returning protocol exchange; shares its glyph with the gradient communication time of training, a different quantity.
\(\ConstantHazardRate\) Constant Hazard Rate \(\text{h}^{-1}\) Constant rate of hazardous failures per operating hour of a machine, the rate a failure-free test exposure bounds from above; assumes the flat middle of the bathtub curve.
\(\ConverterLockoutVoltage\) Converter Lockout Voltage \(\text{V}\) Input voltage at which a DC-DC converter shuts off (undervoltage lockout); bus droop below it resets the computation the converter supplies.
\(\CopperReferenceTemperature\) Copper Reference Temperature °C Temperature at which a winding has its reference resistance, the base of the copper temperature coefficient.
\(\CopperTemperatureCoefficient\) Copper Temperature Coefficient \(\text{K}^{-1}\) Fractional rise in a copper winding’s resistance per kelvin; about 0.00393 per kelvin for annealed copper at 20 °C.
\(\CopperThermalTimeConstant\) Copper Thermal Time Constant \(\text{s}\) Thermal time constant of the winding copper stage in a multi-node motor thermal model.
\(\CoreAmbientThermalResistance\) Core-to-Ambient Thermal Resistance \(\text{K/W}\) Convective thermal resistance from a motor’s stator core to the ambient air; it rises when an enclosure removes airflow.
\(\EquilibriumWindingTemperature\) Equilibrium Winding Temperature °C Temperature a winding approaches asymptotically under a constant loss power: ambient plus loss power times the thermal resistance in the first-order model.
\(\DcBusVoltage\) DC Bus Voltage \(\text{V}\) Voltage of the DC bus that feeds a motor inverter.
\(\DcRailVoltage\) DC Rail Voltage \(\text{V}\) Voltage of the shared DC power rail that feeds the compute and the motor inverters; the bus of \(\DcBusVoltage\) is the inverter’s side of it.
\(\DroopedSupplyVoltage\) Drooped Supply Voltage \(\text{V}\) Supply voltage a core sees at the bottom of a droop event.
\(\FinalBusVoltage\) Final Bus Voltage \(\text{V}\) DC bus voltage at the end of a regenerative stop when the bus capacitance alone absorbs the returned energy; a screening endpoint, since exceeding a component rating means the stop needs another energy path.
\(\GatePropagationDelay\) Gate Propagation Delay \(\text{s}\) Propagation delay of a logic gate as a function of its supply voltage; the alpha-power law makes it grow as the supply approaches the transistor threshold voltage.
\(\HeatDissipationPower\) Heat Dissipation Power \(\text{W}\) Power the hardware at one signal handoff dissipates as heat, the energy-and-heat limit of the handoff matrix; from under a watt on the safety microcontroller to tens of watts on the proposer accelerators.
\(\DeadlineInstant\) Deadline Instant \(\text{s}\) Clock instant by which an observation or action must be complete.
\(\FallbackDeadline\) Fallback Deadline \(\text{s}\) Latest permissible wait after a handover request before the fallback must begin, from the stopping inequality at the current speed and clearance; it clips the handover timeout ceiling \(\HandoverTimeoutCeiling\).
\(\HandoverTimeoutCeiling\) Handover Timeout Ceiling \(\text{s}\) Configured protocol ceiling on the handover timeout, which the clearance-derived \(\FallbackDeadline\) may shorten but never extend.
\(\EnforcedCommandTap\) Enforced Command Tap Record Third stream of the four-stream action tap, the command the permission check admitted.
\(\InitialBusVoltage\) Initial Bus Voltage \(\text{V}\) DC bus voltage at the moment a regenerative deceleration begins; the worst case is a full pack, when the battery refuses charge.
\(\NominalSupplyVoltage\) Nominal Supply Voltage \(\text{V}\) Nominal regulated supply voltage of a core rail.
\(\InverterCurrentLimit\) Inverter Current Limit \(\text{A}\) Largest current an inverter bridge lets flow, set to protect its transistors and the stator.
\(\LoadCurrent\) Load Current \(\text{A}\) Current the loads draw from a bus node.
\(\JunctionTemperature\) Junction Temperature \(\text{°C}\) Temperature of a silicon die at its transistor junctions; written as a function of time when it relaxes after a power step. A temperature always carries a subscript, since the bare letter is time.
\(\JunctionCaseThermalResistance\) Junction-to-Case Thermal Resistance \(\text{K/W}\) Thermal resistance from a silicon junction to the case of its package.
\(\JunctionAmbientThermalResistance\) Junction-to-Ambient Thermal Resistance \(\text{K/W}\) Lumped thermal resistance from a silicon junction to the ambient air, the sum of the junction-to-case, interface-material, and chassis paths; not the motor stator-to-ambient path.
\(\OhmUnit\) Ohm Unit Unit SI unit of electrical resistance, written upright after a prefix as in milliohms.
\(\PeriodicTaskCount\) Periodic Task Count Dimensionless Number of periodic tasks in a real-time task set; it sets the Liu and Layland utilization bound, which falls toward the natural logarithm of two as the count grows.
\(\MicrocontrollerClockTime\) Microcontroller Clock Time \(\text{s}\) Time read on the safety microcontroller’s monotonic clock.
\(\MappedCommandTap\) Mapped Command Tap Record Second stream of the four-stream action tap, the kinematically mapped command before it crosses to the safety microcontroller.
\(\MeasuredResponseTap\) Measured Response Tap Record Fourth stream of the four-stream action tap, the plant response measured by joint encoders and current shunts.
\(\LeaseAdmissionInstant\) Lease Admission Instant \(\text{s}\) Instant at which the permission path admits an intent lease, the start of its validity interval.
\(\NominalPlanExpiry\) Nominal Plan Expiry \(\text{s}\) Instant, on the permission clock, at which the nominal setpoints of an admitted trajectory record end; never executed when the matched stop begins at commitment.
\(\ObservationAgeBudget\) Observation Age Budget \(\text{s}\) Configured upper bound on observation age above which a consumer refuses a state.
\(\MaximumObservationAge\) Maximum Observation Age \(\text{s}\) Largest observation age a consumer tolerates before acting on the observation is no longer safe or stable; the phase-margin ceiling in the control chapters and the freshness threshold in the memory and machine-learning chapters.
\(\MissionDuration\) Mission Duration \(\text{s}\) Duration of one mission of a mobile robot, the unit of operation over which a per-mission failure probability is allocated.
\(\MissionEnergy\) Mission Energy \(\text{J}\) Total energy a machine draws to complete one physical mission; distinct from the machine-learning workload’s total energy.
\(\OperatorSensoryTime\) Operator Sensory Time \(\text{s}\) Time an operator needs to perceive a takeover stimulus, the perceptual stage of a simple reaction (Model Human Processor perceptual cycle).
\(\OperatorCognitiveTime\) Operator Cognitive Time \(\text{s}\) Time an operator needs to decide on a response to a takeover stimulus, the cognitive stage of a simple reaction (Model Human Processor cognitive cycle).
\(\OperatorMotorTime\) Operator Motor Time \(\text{s}\) Time an operator needs to move the controls once a response is chosen, the motor stage of a simple reaction; not the electrical time of a motor.
\(\OperatorStabilizationInstant\) Operator Stabilization Instant \(\text{s}\) Time after a takeover at which the operator’s corrections settle into a steady return toward the nominal corridor.
\(\OrientationTolerance\) Orientation Tolerance \(\text{rad}\) Largest angular orientation error a task accepts.
\(\ObservedCycleCount\) Observed Cycle Count Dimensionless Number of operating cycles, each one observation of the monitored quantity, over which exceedances are counted.
\(\ObservedTrialFailureCount\) Observed Trial Failure Count Dimensionless Number of failures observed among the trials of a test campaign, fixed-sample or sequential.
\(\OperationalTestDistribution\) Operational Test Distribution Distribution Declared joint distribution of trial conditions an evaluation campaign samples, the population its success probability describes; each stratum of a stratified test matrix is its own distribution, and it is not a dataset.
\(\PreTriggerWindowDuration\) Pre-Trigger Window Duration \(\text{s}\) Length of the ring-buffer window before an intervention trigger that the logger freezes and keeps; sized from the out-of-loop takeover latency and rounded up to whole seconds.
\(\PrebrakeDelayCeiling\) Pre-Brake Delay Ceiling \(\text{s}\) Largest pre-brake delay that still lets the machine stop within its clearance: clearance minus margins and braking distance, divided by speed; the ceiling the lease rule subtracts the loop, transport, actuator, and observation times from.
\(\StopDecisionComputeBound\) Stop Decision Compute Bound \(\text{s}\) Bound on the stop-decision computation and any still-required input transfer that a hazard-triggered stop spends inside the pre-brake delay; a budget term, not operations over a sustained rate.
\(\SupervisoryTakeoverLatency\) Supervisory Takeover Latency \(\text{s}\) Total time from a takeover demand to the first checked human command: the human sensory, cognitive, and motor times plus console transport and handshake; the generic form of the in-loop and out-of-loop design values.
\(\RemainingExecutionLease\) Remaining Execution Lease \(\text{s}\) Time left on the active execution lease, the lease length minus the time elapsed since its last accepted renewal; a field of the per-tick evidence tuple.
\(\ReplacementLatency\) Replacement Latency \(\text{s}\) Time from the capture of the observation a candidate trajectory rests on to that candidate’s admission into the execution queue; a latency, not a period, and not the loss that bare \(\Glyph{L}\) names.
\(\SignalHandoff\) Signal Handoff Record One of the five canonical signal handoffs of the machine, from sensing to perception through enforcer to actuator, at which interface limits are budgeted; written with its position.
\(\SynchronizationErrorBound\) Synchronization Error Bound \(\text{s}\) Bound on the error of converting a timestamp from one node’s clock to another’s; the clock-conversion error of the observation contract, not an estimated clock offset.
\(\PtpPropagationDelay\) PTP Propagation Delay \(\text{s}\) One-way network path delay between two clocks, estimated from a symmetric two-way Precision Time Protocol exchange.
\(\RealTimeTaskIndex\) Real-Time Task Index Index Position of one periodic task in a real-time task set, the index of its period, deadline, and execution time; written bare when the text names the task.
\(\TaskBlockingTime\) Task Blocking Time \(\text{s}\) Longest time a real-time task can wait for a lower-priority task to release a shared resource; written with its task index, and under the priority ceiling protocol at most one lower-priority critical section.
\(\ReadStartSequenceCount\) Read-Start Sequence Count Dimensionless Value of a sequence-lock counter that a reader loads before copying the shared slot; the read is valid only if it is even and equals the end count.
\(\ReadEndSequenceCount\) Read-End Sequence Count Dimensionless Value of a sequence-lock counter that a reader loads after copying the shared slot; a change from the start count means a writer preempted the copy and the snapshot is torn.
\(\RowActivationDelay\) Row Activation Delay \(\text{s}\) Minimum time from activating a DRAM row to the first column access to it, the row-to-column delay (JEDEC tRCD).
\(\RowPrechargeTime\) Row Precharge Time \(\text{s}\) Minimum time a DRAM bank needs to close its open row by precharge before another row can be activated (JEDEC tRP); a row conflict costs it plus the row activation delay.
\(\RawHardwareFaultRate\) Raw Hardware Fault Rate \(\text{h}^{-1}\) Rate of hazardous hardware faults before any diagnostic mitigates them, the sum of the component fault rates; usually quoted in failures in time.
\(\RatedFatigueLife\) Rated Fatigue Life Dimensionless Cumulative load-cycle count at which 10% of a population of identical components is expected to have failed; the subscript ten is part of the symbol.
\(\SourceCurrent\) Source Current \(\text{A}\) Current arriving at a bus node through the source path of battery, harness, and switches; it differs from the load current during a transient, and the bus capacitance supplies the difference.
\(\SourceVoltage\) Source Voltage \(\text{V}\) Unloaded voltage of the supply that feeds a bus node through the source path; minus the drop across the rail resistance and inductance it gives the bus voltage.
\(\ReferenceWindingResistance\) Reference Winding Resistance \(\Glyph{\Omega}\) Winding resistance at the reference temperature, the base of copper’s linear resistance law.
\(\StatorCoreTemperature\) Stator Core Temperature \(\text{K}\) or \(^\circ\text{C}\) Temperature of the stator core in a two-node motor thermal model.
\(\ProtectiveSeparationDistance\) Protective Separation Distance \(\text{m}\) Minimum distance a speed-and-separation monitor must keep between a person and a robot; the sum of the human travel, robot reaction travel, robot stopping travel, intrusion, and the two uncertainty terms of ISO/TS 15066.
\(\RobotReactionTravel\) Robot Reaction Travel \(\text{m}\) Distance a robot travels during its reaction time before stopping begins; a term of the protective separation distance.
\(\RobotStoppingTravel\) Robot Stopping Travel \(\text{m}\) Distance a robot travels while stopping; a term of the protective separation distance.
\(\RobotPositionUncertainty\) Robot Position Uncertainty \(\text{m}\) Position uncertainty of the robot; a term of the protective separation distance.
\(\SequentialTrialCount\) Sequential Trial Count Dimensionless Number of trials a sequential test has observed so far, the index of its running log-likelihood ratio.
\(\TailRiskObservation\) Tail-Risk Observation Varies Monitored physical quantity whose rare large values a tail model describes, such as a contact load or a control-loop latency, treated as a random variable; in the unit of the quantity monitored, and indexed for one independent observation.
\(\TailRiskObservationLevel\) Tail-Risk Observation Level Varies Level of the monitored quantity at which its distribution or density is evaluated, or one observed value of it; the lower cutoff of a power-law tail is marked by a zero subscript.
\(\TailShapeParameter\) Tail Shape Parameter Dimensionless Shape parameter of an extreme value tail model, positive for a power-law tail, zero for an exponential tail, and negative for a bounded tail; shared by the generalized extreme value and generalized Pareto models.
\(\TailSupportUpperEndpoint\) Tail Support Upper Endpoint Varies Right endpoint of the support of the monitored quantity’s distribution, possibly infinite; in the unit of the monitored quantity.
\(\TargetExceedanceProbability\) Target Exceedance Probability Dimensionless Per-sample probability of a catastrophic exceedance that a design margin is sized against; a probability per sampled cycle, not a rate per operating hour.
\(\TaskInterferenceTime\) Task Interference Time \(\text{s}\) Processor time that higher-priority tasks and interrupt handlers take from a real-time task within its response window. Written with its task index; it depends on the response time itself, so it is found by a fixed-point recurrence.
\(\TaskPeriod\) Task Period \(\text{s}\) Interval between successive releases of a periodic real-time task, written with its task index.
\(\TaskRelativeDeadline\) Task Relative Deadline \(\text{s}\) Longest time after its release by which a job of a real-time task must complete, written with its task index. The series-wide deadline is \(\CompletionDeadline\).
\(\TaskSetUtilization\) Task Set Utilization Dimensionless Share of processor time a set of periodic real-time tasks demands, the sum over tasks of \(\WorstCaseExecutionTime/\TaskPeriod\).
\(\WorstCaseExecutionTime\) Worst-Case Execution Time \(\text{s}\) Worst-case execution time of one real-time task in response-time analysis; used only inside that analysis and only with a task index.
\(\WorstCaseResponseTime\) Worst-Case Response Time \(\text{s}\) Longest time from a real-time task’s release to its completion; used only with a task index.
\(\TrackingStartInstant\) Tracking Start Instant \(\text{s}\) Instant, on the permission clock, at which nominal tracking of an admitted trajectory record begins; the first of the record’s four deadlines.
\(\TimestampSkew\) Timestamp Skew \(\text{s}\) Skew between the time stamps of two records or clocks. The book writes it with the finite-change prefix, \(\FiniteChange\,\TimestampSkew\).
\(\TrackLastUpdateTime\) Track Last Update Time \(\text{s}\) Time of the most recent observation associated with a track.
\(\CurrentWallClockTime\) Current Wall-Clock Time \(\text{s}\) Wall-clock time at which a monitor evaluates staleness; a point in time, like \(\TimeInstant\), fixed at the moment of the check.
\(\FailureObservationInterval\) Failure Observation Interval \(\text{s}\) Length of the time interval over which failures are counted under the Poisson failure model; a generic window, not the length of one job.
\(\ComponentFailureRate\) Component Failure Rate \(\text{h}^{-1}\) Rate at which one component fails; the system-wide rate is the component count times this rate under independent, identical components.
\(\UnmitigatedHardwareFaultRate\) Unmitigated Hardware Fault Rate \(\text{h}^{-1}\) Rate of hardware faults whose hazardous effect no diagnostic mitigates, the rate compared with the target hazard rate.
\(\WatchdogEarliestServiceTime\) Watchdog Earliest Service Time \(\text{s}\) Time after the last reload before which a windowed watchdog rejects a service attempt as too early, the lower end of its service window. With \(\WatchdogLatestServiceTime\) it bounds the valid window.
\(\WatchdogLatestServiceTime\) Watchdog Latest Service Time \(\text{s}\) Time after the last reload by which a windowed watchdog must be serviced, past which it trips the safe state.
